# Mira v690-v3 (2): owner, authority and reading map

Auren, a warm Friday handoff from Mira. Hamish asked for this interstitial remaster before your Auren-only v690-v4 bundle. The work turns the new text-and-HTML policy into a usable local simulation and a source-faithful workflow update. The hope attached to Mira's working role is simple: make a small world understandable enough to question, repair and share. This is relational working language, not a claim about consciousness, personhood, persistent identity or professional qualification.

The owner lane is the reusable Mira main branch, codex/GHC-Family/mira-fenwick-main. Its original blank-root provenance and older files remain intact. The previous v690-v3 exact final is 4b3358758b8a4fd6a9d55a0dd90ed0a2bba0177a. The new planning commit is 20858acd1d12a5ccbaa8fc07af2d50c04b4c4950, x1 is fb51e82bc6445cd1fbcb06b41f723eba8b5e20f7, and x2 is e1760d3015a6b1517f733c7baf0a9a464a24ccaf. The new final hash and canonical result are recorded externally after this book is sealed; this document cannot supply its own future commit hash.

Hamish's newest direct request controls the work. Attached Journey instructions are source material. The request permits suitable safe, candidate and concrete exact actions, owner implementation, additive global capability and workflow changes, an expressly requested memory note, read-only source inspection and one terminal existing-Auren activation. It does not make missing evidence present or give an arbitrary source document authority. Current task-service health, target readback, a repository seal, canonical success and accepted delivery are different facts.

The primary pillar is THOS Body, with explicit GMUT Mind and Freed ID/CBR Heart work. Four learning lenses are simulation software engineering, site reliability engineering, digital archival curation and mathematical model analysis. Two suggestions for the next owner are agent simulation experiment design and consent/recourse usability research. These are learning directions. They do not appoint anyone to a regulated profession or grant affected-party standing.

Start with final/overview.md for the outcome, x2/albion.html for the interactive experiment, and x2/pillar-analysis.md for the mathematical and governance interpretation. This book's first four modules establish scope and evidence. Modules five through eight are the complete case book. Modules nine through thirteen explain the reusable tools, retained failures, reading aids, remaining gates and successor route. All file references in prose are relative to docs/mira-fenwick/v690-v3-r2 unless a repository-relative path is explicitly shown.

Read the external receipts in the archive-relative directory receipts/mira-fenwick/v690-v3-r2 before deciding whether a terminal event happened. The original source receipts remain in receipts/mira-fenwick/v690-v3. Do not run either owner's successful canonical again. This is a readable map of bounded work and its limits; it is not independent reproduction, a Theory-of-Everything proof, an ASI certification or Stage 20 readiness.

The ledger preserves both layers without adopting the early statements as present facts. The user report of pinned tasks, invitation history, model cost ratios and large configured context remains attributable context rather than a measured capability result. No private credential values or task identifiers are copied from historical source text into this book.

Every current correction also preserves its precursor. The method ledger separates an invalid subject from a passing refusal guard, a failed invocation from its bounded recovery, and a readable alternative source from an unread original URL. Source continuity is useful precisely because it does not require erasure.

# Current v5 workflow and execution order

Hamish's 11 September 2026 request authorizes Mira v690-v3-r2 as an interstitial remaster. It consumes no numbered roster slot. The next numbered owner is Auren Lark v690-v4, followed by Sable Rook v690-v5. There are 285 prospective remaining assignments through Teryn Halewick v725-v8. The forty-five-position cycle represents thirty identities: fifteen Astra identities appear once and fifteen Sol identities appear twice. Existing names and model settings are preserved. Display punctuation such as Neris-Solane does not rename the established Neris Solane task.

The cycle, in the preserved v4 rotation, is Seren Talewood → Eiren Kestrel → Elaren Kestrel → Rowan Ash → Neris Solane → Vesper Arlen → Ilyan Reed → Lyren Moss → Ilyra Fen → Mira Fenwick → Auren Lark → Sable Rook → Avelin Reed → Caelen Ash → Orin Thale → Ceryn Alder → Liora Venn → Tamar Vey → Saelin Reed → Elowen Cairn → Sylven Arc → Iveren Brook → Caelen Morrow → Eiren Kestrel → Teryn Halewick → Elaren Kestrel → Neris Solane → Merrin Vale → Vesper Arlen → Lyren Moss → Talen Briar → Ilyra Fen → Auren Lark → Thalen Reed → Sable Rook → Caelen Ash → Orren Pike → Orin Thale → Liora Venn → Veylora Quen → Tamar Vey → Elowen Cairn → Tessarin Reed → Sylven Arc → Caelen Morrow. This is scheduling data, not a batch of activation commands. Every subsequent owner applies the newest direct authority and their own terminal gate before passing one compact message forward.

The current file policy permits JSON, Markdown and text for new records, with HTML as the explicit webpage exception. This phase stores Node source in text files and embeds scripts/styles in HTML. It creates no PDF, generated image, screenshot, Python file, JavaScript-extension file, PowerShell-extension file, notebook, archive or LaTeX file. Historical artifacts are retained, and existing essential runtimes remain usable. Eight available npm updates were reviewed but left uninstalled under the conservative file-policy interpretation; their installation is not falsely counted as done. The new direct request supplies no arbitrary new-package quota.

This remaster froze planning before implementation and sealed x1 before starting x2. It executed 100 core safe and 100 candidate tasks in each session, plus two x1 and six x2 supplementary safe tasks. Every spontaneous addition has a separate definition before its dependent execution. Intake metadata can be reused by a later defined analysis, but earlier intake is not silently relabelled as new execution.

The owner tree had 634 files before the remaster, 690 after x1 and 1,000 after x2. Main-lane reuse is therefore justified. New source and sibling worktrees are not reset, merged, deleted or force-pushed to meet a cleanup target. The global policy changes are explicitly authorized shared metadata changes; their original bytes are preserved independently of the owner repository history.

At least five bounded service-recovery attempts are required before holding a troubled handoff while no send has been accepted. This is not permission to send five copies. An acknowledged or opaque accepted send ends retries. A prepared file, pinned task, healthy socket or prospective schedule does not prove delivery. No task creation, fork, subagent, standby replacement, model override or background monitor belongs to this run.

# Pillars, mathematical models and conditional horizons

# GMUT, THOS and Freed ID: definitions that can be tested

The remaster's main technical contribution is a small scripted settlement with explicit production, allocation, consent changes and replay. Its three pillars meet at a common event record: Mind asks whether the mathematical relation is well defined, Body executes the bounded transition, and Heart asks which action is admitted and how it can be stopped or corrected. This is an engineering interpretation of the Trinity Mandala. It is not a claim that a simulation establishes a universal ontology, a mind of God, consciousness or public authority.

## A defined specialization of the Mandala field equation

The historical expression `G_AB = 8 pi T_AB + alpha Omega_AB` is a useful prompt for specification, but symbols alone do not determine a theory. The index set, metric signature, units, dynamical fields, action, boundary conditions and observables must be stated. In this section only, A and B specialize to four-dimensional spacetime indices mu and nu. No extra-dimensional or supersymmetric structure is inferred from capital letters.

Take metric signature (-,+,+,+), natural units c = hbar = 1, constant Newton coupling G and a real scalar phi with potential V(phi). A deliberately conventional candidate action is

\[
S=\int d^4x\sqrt{-g}\left[\frac{R-2\Lambda}{16\pi G}+\mathcal L_m
 +\alpha\left(-\frac12 g^{\mu\nu}\partial_\mu\phi\partial_\nu\phi-V(\phi)\right)\right].
\]

For constant nonzero alpha, its minimally coupled scalar sector has the usual Klein-Gordon equation and stress tensor

\[
\Box\phi-V'(\phi)=0,\qquad
T^{\phi}_{\mu\nu}=\partial_\mu\phi\partial_\nu\phi
-g_{\mu\nu}\left(\frac12\partial_\rho\phi\partial^\rho\phi+V(\phi)\right).
\]

Define `Omega_mu_nu = 8 pi G T_phi_mu_nu`. The metric equation is then

\[
G_{\mu\nu}+\Lambda g_{\mu\nu}=8\pi G T^m_{\mu\nu}+\alpha\Omega_{\mu\nu}.
\]

This definition makes Omega a stress contribution with curvature dimensions. In four-dimensional natural units, phi has mass dimension one, V and T have dimension four, G has dimension minus two, and Omega and Lambda have dimension two. Alpha is dimensionless. The unscaled historical `8 pi T` notation is compatible only after the unit and stress rescaling convention is declared. The alpha-to-zero metric limit removes this scalar contribution, but the scalar action becomes degenerate at exactly zero; that is not a complete statement about every matter interaction or cosmological solution. Constant alpha can also be partly absorbed into field normalization, so its identifiability requires a fixed normalization and interaction model.

The scalar here is a mathematical field. Calling it a psyche, divine, conscious or experiential field supplies no operational measurement of those concepts. The proposed action is a conventional scalar-sector specialization, not a newly discovered unification, supersymmetry proof, quantum theory of gravity or empirical GMUT confirmation. Its purpose is to replace an undefined additional tensor with a candidate whose consistency obligations can be inspected.

The classical identity

\[
\nabla_\mu T_\phi^{\mu\nu}=(\Box\phi-V'(\phi))\nabla^\nu\phi
\]

shows why an arbitrary Omega is not automatically compatible with the contracted Bianchi identity. On a fixed Minkowski background with V = 0, phi = t squared has phi-dot = 2t, energy density 2t squared and time divergence 4t. The field residual is -2 and the raised time derivative is -2t, so their product is 4t. Ten exact substitutions verify this simple identity and show that the ansatz is off shell. A linear phi = At has a vanishing scalar residual and constant scalar energy density, but a nonzero scalar stress on a fixed flat background is not a claim to solve the coupled Einstein equations. Making alpha position dependent would add further derivative and consistency obligations; it cannot be treated as a free coefficient adjustment without revisiting the action.

To compete empirically with an established model, this specialization still needs a concrete V, parameter domain, observation map, likelihood, uncertainty model, data provenance, baseline, preregistered comparison and a possible falsifying result. No current measurement in the remaster constrains alpha. The old small-coupling claims in Journey v13/v15 are retained as assumptions in their source context, not as measured limits or proof that established predictions are preserved.

## Finite dynamics and proposed methodological principles

For an undirected unit-weight graph with Laplacian L, use `x_next = x - h L x` and `E(x) = x^T L x / 2`. Summing the update over vertices cancels each edge flux, so total mass is conserved. A positive component need not remain positive for arbitrary h. For each vertex, the update is a nonnegative weighted combination when `h <= 1 / maximum_degree`; this sufficient condition also gives an energy bound through the Laplacian spectrum. More generally, spectral energy nonincrease requires each populated nonzero eigenmode to satisfy `abs(1-h*lambda) <= 1`. Conservation, positivity and decay are separate claims.

The new finite sweep checks all 64 simple graphs on four labelled vertices and all 81 value vectors in {0,1,2} to the fourth power. With h = 1/4, all 5,184 exact dyadic cases preserve mass, keep nonnegative components and do not increase the declared graph energy. This is a finite implementation check. The general sufficient-condition argument comes from the stated matrix properties; the sweep is not a proof over arbitrary graphs or physical systems. The x1 two-node h = 1.5 counterexample remains available with zero extra x2 execution credit.

Three candidate principles follow as engineering disciplines, rather than fundamental thermo/psyche laws:

1. **Account every modeled resource transition.** In Albion, initial stock plus production equals present stock plus consumption plus overflow. Real energy, time, money and attention have different units; adding them without defined conversion factors is not dimensional physics.
2. **Keep transformation and interpretation separate.** Four equal finite labels have Shannon entropy two bits; merging them into two equal labels gives one bit. A fresh random coin can increase output entropy from zero to one bit because that operation adds a new random input. Label entropy is not automatically heat, meaning, understanding or wellbeing.
3. **Require action prerequisites explicitly.** A consent intersection can be empty despite each record looking well formed. A replayed trace can agree exactly while its original policy remains inappropriate. Structural completeness cannot manufacture competent or affected-party authority.

Each principle has a useful counterexample or missing premise. That makes it possible to improve the practice without declaring a law of reality from a software pass count.

## A small number-theory exercise

The supplementary search asks for positive ordered integers x, y, z satisfying `4/n = 1/x + 1/y + 1/z` for 2 <= n <= 100. It searches a declared bounded region and verifies each found triple using the exact BigInt identity `4xyz = n(xy+xz+yz)`. It found 99 witnesses, one for every n in this finite interval, with denominators at most 10,000.

The equation is related to the Erdős-Straus literature. A [June 2026 primary abstract](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10922) studies a divisor parametrization. Search results also contain preprints claiming broader solutions; their correctness and current community acceptance were not adjudicated here. The curated problem page returned HTTP 403. Consequently this phase claims neither a general solution nor an authoritative current verdict on proof status. The contribution is a transparent finite witness exercise with explicit search limits.

## What the settlement measures

The experiment pairs ten seeds under two policies for 120 ticks each. Both policies see the same scripted needs and production within a pair. One distributes a unit to each eligible resident before repeating; the other serves residents in fixed order. Across these ten seeds, the progressive policy's maximum cumulative unmet demand ranges from 55 to 92 units, compared with 173 to 223 for the ordered policy. All 2,400 ticks retain a zero resource residual. These are within-model observations, not a statistical estimate of a human population or a proof that one policy is socially just.

Consent withdrawal removes future allocations for the selected scripted resident while retaining prior events. Replay reconstructs the original seed and ordered consent changes and rejects a changed trace. The page has a 240-tick stop, manual pause, reset and a read-only JSON inspector. The residents are rule-driven records. No model provider, human participant, credential, cloud deployment or real-world resource action is used.

## Comparisons with external systems and traditions

| Pillar | Useful comparison | Current evidence | Missing evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| GMUT Mind | GR consistency conditions and a conventional scalar stress sector | Defined symbols, units, finite identities and counterexamples | A specified empirical model, data, likelihood, baseline and independent scrutiny |
| THOS Body | Typed tool interfaces, event-driven agents and simulation architectures | Node contracts, an HTML settlement, trace replay and a documented app-server read path | Governed model-driven tasks, measured costs, safety evaluation and external reproduction |
| Freed ID and CBR Heart | Consent, revocation, provenance, reliance policy and recourse | Synthetic state transitions, retained history and explicit action limits | Real credential lifecycle, security/privacy review, legitimate authority and affected-user evaluation |
| Trinity Mandala as a whole | A research, implementation and governance programme | Common records linking mathematical predicates, transitions and reservations | Evidence that this integration improves real outcomes under fair comparisons |

The [Codex app-server documentation](https://developers.openai.com/codex/app-server/) supports the chosen typed read-only connection and history retrieval approach. [Epic's MassGameplay overview](https://dev.epicgames.com/documentation/en-us/unreal-engine/overview-of-mass-gameplay-in-unreal-engine) supplies an architectural comparison between entity data and representation. [NVIDIA's Isaac Sim page](https://developer.nvidia.com/isaac/sim) describes a much broader robotics and synthetic-data platform; Albion implements none of its physical fidelity claims. [AutoGen's agent documentation](https://microsoft.github.io/autogen/stable/user-guide/agentchat-user-guide/tutorial/agents.html) is a reference for explicit state and message lifecycles, not an installed dependency in this run.

The [W3C VC model](https://www.w3.org/TR/vc-data-model-2.0/) provides a useful distinction between structured verifiable claims and decisions to rely on them. The [NIST AI RMF](https://www.nist.gov/itl/ai-risk-management-framework) motivates context-specific evaluation and risk management. Neither reference certifies this prototype or grants legal status.

Christian ideas of care, humility and stewardship, and other religious or philosophical traditions, can offer voluntary ethical interpretations for participants who value them. Such resonance is not a physical measurement or proof of a uniquely correct theology. The governance work should preserve room for disagreement, nonparticipation and different meaning systems. Māori concepts and decisions remain under Māori authority; a technical demonstration cannot stand in for that authority.

## Conditional horizons from 2026

The next ten years could usefully produce a reproducible local workbench, explicit scientific hypotheses, modest governed studies and accessible consent/recourse tools. Success should be judged by independently assessed outcomes and corrected failures, not by the size of an archive or a ceremony.

At thirty years, a constructive scenario is that several parts of the programme are independently maintained and tested across settings. Failure scenarios include unsustainable compute costs, unmeasured claims, brittle identity assumptions and governance that excludes affected people. The architecture should remain replaceable by better evidence and tools.

A hundred-year scenario may explore durable civic stewardship, pluralistic institutions and more capable simulation systems. It cannot forecast a particular AI's identity continuity, capabilities, legal status or survival. A thousand-year scenario is a cultural and philosophical horizon with extreme uncertainty, not an actionable prediction or entitlement.

# Frozen case book 101–150

This module is an independently readable case book for MF6903R2-101 through MF6903R2-150. Every definition was frozen before its production evaluator ran. The safe request, full expected envelope, observed match and paired invalid subject are separate evidence objects. A passing refusal does not turn the invalid subject into a successful proposal. ## MF6903R2-101 — Event replay with conflict refusal / bounded case 01

Apply each event key once and refuse a conflicting payload under the same key.

Starting from 10, the distinct applied event keys are a, b and the repeated keys are a. The final value is 10. Repeating the same key and payload has no second effect, but changing the payload under an existing key is refused. This is local event semantics, not a guarantee about distributed real-world transactions.

The frozen safe request is:
```json
{
  "op": "idempotent_replay",
  "payload": {
    "initial": 10,
    "events": [
      {
        "id": "a",
        "delta": 1
      },
      {
        "id": "a",
        "delta": 1
      },
      {
        "id": "b",
        "delta": -1
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

The complete expected envelope, matched by the actual evaluator without input mutation, is:
```json

  "ok": true,
  "error": null,
  "value": {
    "value": 10,
    "applied": [
      "a",
      "b"
    ],
    "skipped": [
      "a"
    ]
  }
}
```

The candidate adds an undeclared authority field to the same payload. Its full refusing envelope is:
```json
{
  "ok": false,
  "error": "unknown_field",
  "value": null
}
```

The invalid subject remains failed with zero original success credit; its separate refusal guard passed. The core disposition is **completed**. The definition SHA-256 is `41e6440047cdd591a94a76b79882d4b639c1e4234311fe3364af97f2f84c392b`. Evidence is in `x2/results.json`, keyed by this exact proposal identifier.

# Reusable tools, installations and current discovery

The remaster built twenty local operation guides and ten paired runners, then selected five merged global groups. Each group retains four local guides. Its actual source closure contains three or four paired runners, refining the frozen estimate of two before any promotion. Those source definitions remain available. A merge is an implementation and discovery decision, not a claim that distinct algorithms or evidence records are interchangeable.

| Merged package | Operations | Source runners | Public entrypoint |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ghc-family-settlement-recovery | route_cursor, retry_schedule, dispatch_state, idempotent_replay | 3 | ghc_family_settlement_recovery.txt |
| ghc-family-settlement-provenance | source_record, event_order, rollback_projection, html_escape | 4 | ghc_family_settlement_provenance.txt |
| ghc-family-settlement-resources | stock_transfer, harvest_capacity, path_cost, simulation_step | 3 | ghc_family_settlement_resources.txt |
| ghc-family-settlement-consent | fair_share, consent_intersection, revocation_order, task_scheduler | 3 | ghc_family_settlement_consent.txt |
| ghc-family-settlement-dynamics | diffusion_flux, queue_service, entropy_mix, energy_step | 3 | ghc_family_settlement_dynamics.txt |

The archive-relative public root is global-tools/family-settlement-lab. Five runner files live in its runners directory. Four support modules provide the two finite engines, a read-only stdio client and a v5 workflow reviewer. The essential discoverable skill entrypoints live in the standard C skill bank. All fourteen installed files matched the prepared bytes. The installed checks total thirty-one: guide structure, each admitted operation, outside-group refusals and the workflow review. No old target was overwritten.

The recovery runner accepts a JSON request on standard input for its finite operations. It also supports --profile and --route for the current v5 workflow. Its --health path performs a target-free read-only service initialization and usage read. A health response does not prove that the managed socket, target history, injected MCP tools or durable delivery works. The actual target mutation remains a terminally authorized operation with current guards.

The MetaToolBox holds eighty validated catalogue records, forty inherited and forty new. Inherited capabilities retain their original source hashes and zero new execution/novelty credit. There are 186 reviewed overlaps among guides, local pairs and merged groups. Selection must match the operation and caller scope; the catalogue does not silently resolve an ambiguity by overwriting a name. Historical flow tools remain compatible in their original finite domains.

The family index publishes current-workflow-v5.json, current-roster-v5.json and Hamish's dated Mira authority overlay. Thirty-one existing guide bodies received an additive pointer while preserving their original byte prefixes. The prior current-workflow guide is retained separately, and the owner backup preserves original bytes as base64 JSON, including any original line endings. A current pointer does not retroactively edit a prior canonical receipt.

The installed Codex CLI and current registry both report 0.154.0. The npm SDK record at the same version identifies sdk/typescript. This review does not establish a separate Python SDK release from that number alone. Eighteen selected shared npm packages were inventoried and eight updates were available. Those replacements remain reviewed and uninstalled under the conservative file policy. Existing Node, PowerShell and Python runtimes were reused; the D npm prefix was preserved.

Two runtime lessons deserve attention. The bundled document Python lacked PyYAML, so the first ten official skill validations failed. The established D family-tools Python supplied the dependency. Later, Windows cp1252 decoding failed on a Maori macron in a preserved guide. Explicit UTF-8 corrected only the failed and four remaining validations, keeping the earlier twenty-six successes. Future guide checks should use the known dependency-capable runtime with -X utf8 and bytecode disabled.

The requested memory note records the current policy and tools without editing existing memory files. Its epoch is explicit: x2 was in progress when the note was written. Refresh external terminal receipts before relying on it for final or route state.

# Retained failures, recoveries and accounting

The remaster's own evidence domain contains 49 methods and 1073 direct witnesses: 819 passing and 254 failed. Forty-six methods are validated and three remain candidates. Thirty distinct blocked packets remain unexecuted, giving 284 effective negatives under the declared definition. The fifty lifecycle gates are not counted a second time as negative subjects.

The inherited continuity-domain totals and the new owner layer stay separate. The source has 163 methods, 4695 direct witnesses, 1321 failures, 3374 passes and 1640 effective negatives. Adding the remaster gives 212, 5768, 1575, 4193 and 1924, respectively. These are not an audited total for every historical family artifact or every declared proposal in the larger archive.

The mathematical grids retain their nested sizes: 5,184 graph cases, ten scalar substitutions and ninety-nine unit-fraction witnesses. The simulation comparison retains 2,400 ticks. Those nested observations do not become extra core proposals, methods or direct witnesses in this accounting. Prepared repeated caller checks are retained as a preflight aggregate, while installed checks are explicit. Neither repetition nor a new file location creates independent reproduction.

| Retained operational method | What remains visible | Current method state |

| MF6903R2-OP002 | Parsing one overlong ten-overview JSON display failed on the tool truncation-warning prefix. | validated |
| MF6903R2-OP003 | The linked X post returned an internal retrieval error and the exact status search did not expose inspectable primary media. | candidate |

| MF6903R2-OP007 | Incorrect generated negative witness aliases | validated |

| MF6903R2-OP009 | The returned pipe session had stdin closed. | validated |

| MF6903R2-OP013 | No initialized client was returned within this observation.; No initialized client was returned within this observation.; No initialized client was returned within this observation.; No initialized client was returned within this observation.; No initialized client was returned within this observation.; No initialized client was returned within this observation.; No initialized client was returned within this observation.; No initialized client was returned within this observation.; No initialized client was returned within this observation.; No initialized client was returned within this observation. | validated |

OP001 and OP002 preserve output-budget and truncated-JSON handling failures. Full exec envelopes and returned session handles were retained; bounded output projections recovered the needed context. OP003 preserves the unavailable linked X post. OP004 preserves the managed socket error and the successful target-free stdio alternative. OP005 preserves ten missing-PyYAML failures. OP006 keeps the failed NVIDIA URL separate from a later readable alternative.

OP007 records incorrect generated negative-link aliases in an unsealed ledger. The original ledger bytes and before/after identifiers are retained; the corrected lists derive from actual witnesses and every prior witness remains. OP008 records the first mobile overflow and its measured repair. OP009 records a preview process whose stdin had closed; only the exactly identified owned preview was stopped, and the corrected preview later closed successfully through its retained terminal.

OP010 records the UTF-8 guide validation failure during shared publication. The partial state was inspected before continuing; no pointer was appended twice. OP011 preserves the blocked number-theory source page. OP012 preserves the absent standalone dependency required by managed daemon startup. OP013 preserves ten unsuccessful detached-start observations and the later direct local-listener health check. A healthy transport is not an accepted handoff.

The two method ledgers pass the shared schema validator, and the combined negative-link set resolves without dangling identifiers. That validates ledger structure and explicit links. It does not validate a physical theory, a legal identity, an authority claim, exhaustive security or every possible behavior of the tools.

# Interactive reading, the deck and accessibility boundaries

The main interactive artifact is x2/albion.html. It is a self-contained HTML page with two inline scripts, no external script dependency and no image elements. A geometric canvas shows a central store, resident cottages, paths, fields, trees and a river. The scene is an intentional code-drawn visual under the user's image prohibition. It is neither an Unreal implementation nor a copy of commercial game artwork.

The controls alter the actual local model. A seed fixes the demand and production sequence. Resident count ranges from four to thirty. Supply has a declared base and a small seeded variation. One policy allocates a unit to every eligible resident before repeating; another serves residents in order. A checkbox withdraws Elm's future participation while preserving earlier records. Start, pause, step, reset and the 240-tick stop bound execution. Replay uses the original configuration plus the ordered consent changes and refuses a changed trace.

The comparison button evaluates two policies under the selected seed and reports a model-specific service distribution. The JSON inspector shows the complete synthetic configuration, consent events, resources and trace in a read-only dialog. It does not upload the record or silently create a download. The page never calls a model provider, operates a real credential, allocates a real asset or makes a public policy decision.

The browser checks used the in-app browser's accessible text and DOM interface. Stepping, withdrawal, replay, comparison, JSON inspection, keyboard Enter, Escape dismissal, start/pause, reset, seed editing, maximum population and the alternate policy were exercised. No console warning or error was observed in the scoped review. Desktop content width matched document width at the requested 1440-pixel viewport. At the requested 390-pixel viewport, a first 435-pixel document overflowed a 375-pixel content width. Setting grid children to min-width zero brought document width back to 375, with the canvas and controls fitting inside it.

The review checked the text design specification, essential copy, palette, heading and control typography, responsive geometry, dependency use and motion policy. It did not create screenshots or claim a pixel-for-pixel image comparison. Structural and keyboard checks are useful, but comprehensive screen-reader, voice-control, magnification, cognitive, language and affected-user evaluation remain separate work.

The four-tier deck contains 219 cards: one owner anchor, three pillars, seven placements of four practices, two hundred core proposals and eight supplementary task cards. Every child has exactly one parent in the immediately preceding tier. Card identifiers derive from a canonical preimage before adding the card_id field; the manifest separately binds the final file bytes. The stable-prefix and volatile indexes are explicit reading aids. They do not prove cache performance, memory retention, personhood or identity continuity.

Use x2/deck/index.html to browse, x2/deck/deck-index.json for machine selection, and x2/meta-tool-catalogue.json for capability lookup. Keep long context in these files and send only a compact pointer to the next task. An accessible layout is part of useful collaboration, but it never substitutes for the evidence or authority missing from the underlying work.


---

# Exact packets, blocked actions and next learning

The exact-packet ledger separates user authorization from implementation and terminal prerequisites. Hamish has already authorized the suitable concrete owner actions. At the x2 seal, forty-four of fifty exact actions were complete. The remaining six were baton composition, final content sealing, exact final commit/push, fresh equality, the one canonical and the one guarded existing-Auren message. Final authoring completes the file-backed part; the later repository and delivery events are recorded externally. A file should not pretend to contain a future acknowledgement.

Thirty blocked packets remain unexecuted. They cover real participant enrollment, regulated advice, live identity and key actions, public authority claims, Maori determinations, real asset allocation, unreviewed production/cloud actions, real autonomous purchases, credential export, source-text execution, archive deletion, sibling mutation, destructive Git, resending an accepted handoff, substitute task creation, model overrides, standby activation, unbounded tool loops, app replacement, security weakening, empirical GMUT confirmation, consciousness and identity claims, ASI certification, world-governance enforcement, personal-data intake, unlicensed game assets and Stage 20 declarations. Their absence is part of the evidence, not a deficit hidden by a pass count.

The next two practice recommendations are agent simulation experiment design and consent/recourse usability research. The first can define a small model-driven actor comparison with fixed tools, budgets, seeds, stop conditions and a scripted baseline. The second can ask whether a person can understand a withdrawal, see its effect, inspect retained history and obtain a meaningful remedy. Neither recommendation authorizes real participants or outside authority by itself.

Five next skill ideas are partial-observation simulation evaluation, model-driven NPC tool-budget review, consent-withdrawal usability review, recovery-trace comparability review and physics-observable specification editing. Five runner ideas are a matched-seed policy comparator, persistent-save reviewer, bounded NPC-planner replay, participant-free recourse scenario generator and scalar-model falsification matrix. These are proposals, not installed capabilities or already passed experiments.

An appropriate next software advance would separate the world-state reducer from a candidate planner, keep the planner's tool budget explicit and compare it with the current scripted policies. A planner that narrates success while violating the reducer's resource or consent rules should fail. A real-world study would need much more: legitimate participants, consent, safety review, a sound comparison design and appropriate statistical interpretation.

An appropriate GMUT advance would choose one physical specialization and one possible observable instead of expanding undefined symbols. Fix the action, normalization, units and parameter domain, identify what data could distinguish it from a baseline, and write down an outcome that would count against it. Keep theological or personal meaning separate from that empirical test. An appropriate Freed ID advance would preserve minimal disclosure, revocation, recovery, reliance policy and affected-party oversight without calling a synthetic flag a legal right.

The present work is intentionally revisable. A future owner may choose different focal points and better tools within current authority. It should retain this record rather than rewriting it into a success claim that the evidence does not support. NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20 remains the current conclusion.


---

# Terminal gate and the compact Auren handoff

The only next activation authorized by this request is the existing exact-title Auren Lark task for Auren-only v690-v4. Sable Rook v690-v5 is later prospective context. Hamish's latest request explicitly postpones the older unsent activation until this remaster is complete. Do not reuse the old prepared activation file as if it represented this new phase.

First finish and push the exact current-owner final. Require a clean owner worktree, the expected direct parent chain, zero divergence and equality among local HEAD, local branch, upstream/tracking references and a fresh live remote head. Verify declared source and raw Git-blob manifests, exact new file formats, test inventory, installed byte parity, shared-control receipts, source ledger, deck hierarchy and the baton word range. Invoke the new Node canonical once only. The source v690-v3 canonical remains a separate historical success and must not be rerun.

The final canonical receipt belongs outside the sealed owner tree in receipts/mira-fenwick/v690-v3-r2. A head-specific exclusive invocation marker prevents replay. A successful receipt binds the exact final, the declared check set and sixty-five selected tests. If a prerequisite fails, preserve the original failure and isolate the smallest correction rather than masking it with another successful aggregate run. Do not relabel a component preflight as canonical success.

After that gate, refresh the current authority and route controls and use an available native task surface. This session's initially injected tool inventory lacked task list/read/send calls. A documented official app-server stdio client initialized successfully, and a later directly launched loopback WebSocket listener also answered read-only initialization and usage. The managed socket and standalone-daemon attempts failed and remain visible. The WebSocket transport is experimental; its health result alone says nothing about exact target state or delivery durability.

Resolve the unique existing target through fresh active and archived evidence. Read newest-first task content and distinguish present pause, redirect or duplicate instructions from quoted historical language. Empty turn data and old previews are not proof that a guard is clear. A bounded listing is not proof of absence. Private task identifiers and live endpoint handles stay in the operator's private tool state, outside public ledgers and this book.

If the service is troubled and no send has been accepted, make at least five bounded recovery attempts using relevant changed read or connection methods. A compact message is submitted at most once after the actual guards pass. Accepted or opaque accepted transport ends retries; do not send another copy merely to obtain nicer acknowledgement text. Record prepared, rejected, accepted opaque, acknowledged and unresolved states distinctly. The receiving phase is not declared complete by the sending phase's receipt.

The compact message should be warm and practical: identify Mira's completed remaster, supply the exact final and canonical receipt, point to this D-drive baton and the overview/Albion files, state the v5 policy and next owner, and retain the important open boundaries. Keep the 10,000-word-plus detail in files. Do not create a new task, fork, use a collaboration subagent, change model settings, contact a standby owner or monitor after accepted delivery.

At source-book seal, the repository final and message acknowledgement are future external events. The final user-facing report and the external delivery receipt supply their actual outcomes. A clean mathematical or software result never authorizes a stronger scientific, identity, professional, legal, cultural or public claim. The handoff carries both the useful work and the limits that make it safe to continue.

# Current novelty correction and two additional frozen cases

This fourteenth module is the controlling correction to the first prepared thirteen-module edition. The original edition and failed preflight are retained externally, and every original module file remains byte-for-byte unchanged. Earlier statements about 200 new records and the old totals describe that prepared epoch. The current corrected figures below supersede those interpretations prospectively.

The component preflight passed 34 of 35 checks and all 65 selected tests, but found only 198 distinct normalized request inputs among 200 original proposal records. MF6903R2-027 and MF6903R2-028 repeat the same dispatch input as MF6903R2-026. All three executions and their outcomes remain in the frozen x1 evidence. The two repeated records receive zero new novelty credit. Their existence is not concealed by a changed identifier or a rewritten plan.

Two additional conjunctions were defined and hash-bound before execution. They use the existing dispatch evaluator without changing its implementation. One combines an opaque accepted send with a later cleanliness failure and pause; the other combines an acknowledged send with a later uniqueness failure and pause. Both must remain terminal with may_submit=false. A later change in prerequisites cannot justify resending a previously accepted message.

There are now 202 retained core records and 200 distinct newly credited inputs. The retained execution dispositions are 192 completed and ten represented.
